# Old artifact cleanup plan — read-only inventory

Audit date: 2026-10-19. Root: `E:/Cancerdetection/EG-VAN`. **This document is a proposal only.** No artifact was deleted, moved, overwritten, restored, preprocessed, split, or trained. The nine-class overlap evidence remains protected. All sizes below are logical bytes at audit time; a missing file's size comes from the matching Git blob, not the current filesystem.

## Decision rules and recovery standard

Categories: **REQUIRED_FOR_NINE_CLASS** = input or evidence necessary to define the new dataset; **REUSABLE_AFTER_VALIDATION** = a derived artifact that might be useful only if its transformations, source, and integrity match the approved protocol; **HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS** = a seven-class experiment/result artifact, not an input to the new nine-class task; **UNKNOWN_OR_DEPENDENCY** = role or provenance unresolved, or required by an existing historical consumer. Decisions: **KEEP** in place, **ARCHIVE** only as a future approval candidate with verified copy/dependencies, **REVIEW** before any storage decision.

“Recoverable from GitHub: yes” means the exact path, byte size, and Git blob are present on the [historical repository's current `main` tree](https://github.com/prasanna14200/Skin_cancer_detection_using_dual_network/tree/main/EG-VAN), and the local tracked blob agrees where checked. It is still prudent to pin a commit and verify the recovered SHA-256 before a future archive action. The configured local `origin` is a *different* repository, `prasanna14200/Skin_cancer_detection`; no recoverability claim relies on that origin. “No” means the named path is absent from that historical GitHub tree; source-code regeneration is **not** byte-for-byte recovery. “Unknown” means no exact remote copy was established. No download or restoration was performed.

## Inventory summary

| Local path | Present now | Files / size | Classification | Decision |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- | --- |
| `data/splits/` | **No**; both CSVs show as tracked deletions | 2 historical blobs / 703,101 B | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | KEEP historical evidence; do not recreate for nine-class |
| `data/processed/images/` | Yes | 10,015 JPG / 1,266,395,525 B | REUSABLE_AFTER_VALIDATION | KEEP; validate before reuse or any archive discussion |
| `data/processed/metadata_clean.csv` | Yes | 573,293 B | REUSABLE_AFTER_VALIDATION | REVIEW |
| `data/processed/preprocessing_log.csv` | Yes | 1,819,890 B | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | KEEP |
| `data/processed/preprocessing_samples/` | Yes | 20 JPG / 3,428,225 B | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | ARCHIVE candidate, not approved |
| `data/metadata/` | **No** | 0 current files | — | No action |
| `checkpoints/` | Yes | 1 PT / 81,652,547 B | UNKNOWN_OR_DEPENDENCY | REVIEW; retain in place |
| `analysis/nine_class_similarity/` | Yes | 2 CSV / 5,311,347 B | REQUIRED_FOR_NINE_CLASS | KEEP |
| `models/frozen_stage23/` | Yes | 5 files / 28,998 B | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | KEEP |

No local historical evaluation-result files were found under the six inspected folders. Historical seven-class evaluation/results appear in the remote `analysis/` and `experiments/` trees, which are **not present locally**; do not infer their local sizes or restore them for nine-class work. The existing docs' references to `data/splits/` describe a previous state: both CSVs are absent now. This audit neither caused nor repaired those deletions.

## File decisions and references

All paths in this table are exact project-relative paths. “GitHub yes” is supported by the [historical split listing](https://github.com/prasanna14200/Skin_cancer_detection_using_dual_network/tree/main/EG-VAN/data/splits), [processed listing](https://github.com/prasanna14200/Skin_cancer_detection_using_dual_network/tree/main/EG-VAN/data/processed), or [frozen model listing](https://github.com/prasanna14200/Skin_cancer_detection_using_dual_network/tree/main/EG-VAN/models/frozen_stage23). The two missing split files have Git blob IDs `578bbb6f84bcdf0cc74a05147da34b07f82d907f` and `51450362c34cd5780dfa0b4cbe3687ea956353c8`, respectively, matching local `HEAD` and historical GitHub.

| Exact path | Bytes | Purpose / direct references | Category | GitHub exact copy | Decision |
| --- | ---: | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `data/splits/split_leakage_aware.csv` (**missing**) | 351,559 | Frozen seven-class HAM split; pinned by `models/frozen_stage23/model_registry.json`, used by `src/run_quality_analysis.py`, `src/run_uncertainty_analysis.py`, baseline `src/train.py`/`src/evaluate.py` via arguments, and historical docs. Prior audit reported 8,015/986/1,014 train/val/test with no HAM lesion crossing. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes**, blob verified | **KEEP** as historical provenance; no nine-class reuse |
| `data/splits/split_naive.csv` (**missing**) | 351,542 | Seven-class HAM baseline split; default of `src/run_baseline.py`, produced by root `build_splits.py`; prior audit found 764 HAM lesion IDs crossing partitions. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes**, blob verified | **ARCHIVE** candidate only after old baseline dependencies are closed; no nine-class reuse |
| `data/processed/metadata_clean.csv` | 573,293 | Seven-class HAM cleaned metadata, written by root `prepare_metadata.py`, input to root `build_splits.py` and `src/run_quality_analysis.py`. Prior audit found 10,015 rows and values matching raw HAM metadata. | REUSABLE_AFTER_VALIDATION | **Yes**, tracked and historical remote listing matches size | **REVIEW**; prefer raw `data/raw/HAM10000_metadata.csv` as new manifest source |
| `data/processed/preprocessing_log.csv` | 1,819,890 | Record of 10,015 historical HAM preprocessing successes, written by `src/preprocessing.py`; cited in `docs/NINE_CLASS_DATA_PIPELINE.md` and inventory. Logged `D:` paths are stale on this machine. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes**, tracked and remote size matches | **KEEP** as provenance for the existing cache |
| `checkpoints/efficientnetv2s_leakage_aware_best.pt` | 81,652,547 | Baseline-named training checkpoint; `src/run_baseline.py` writes same-form checkpoint names, while `src/evaluate.py` and `src/run_uncertainty_analysis.py` accept checkpoint paths. No exact-code reference binds this particular basename to a run. SHA-256 `f96ebe86ffaa984333105c9092ac16e8cc2137190a36411cc0b6445620960848`. Safe `torch.load(weights_only=True)` rejected an unsupported `TorchVersion` global, so internal class count/config was **not** verified; no unsafe deserialization was attempted. It is **not** the missing Stage 23 checkpoint. | UNKNOWN_OR_DEPENDENCY | **No verified GitHub copy**; `checkpoints/` absent from historical tree and file untracked locally | **REVIEW / retain** until provenance, safe inspection, backup, and consumers are resolved |
| `analysis/nine_class_similarity/fingerprints.csv` | 1,080,292 | 13,312 original HAM and canonical binary pHash/dHash fingerprints; emitted by `src/nine_class_similarity_audit.py`, cited by `docs/NINE_CLASS_DATA_PIPELINE.md`. | REQUIRED_FOR_NINE_CLASS | **No** at same historical path; local tracked copy exists | **KEEP**, protected audit record |
| `analysis/nine_class_similarity/candidates.csv` | 4,231,055 | 41,381 perceptual candidate pairs; essential review trail for cross-source duplicate and lesion-risk decisions; same references. | REQUIRED_FOR_NINE_CLASS | **No** at same historical path; local tracked copy exists | **KEEP**, protected audit record |
| `models/frozen_stage23/FREEZE_README.md` | 4,525 | Seven-class Stage 23 freeze/evaluation-boundary description; references registry, manifest, missing checkpoint and audit. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes** | **KEEP** with freeze record |
| `models/frozen_stage23/freeze_manifest.json` | 5,868 | Pinned seven-class hashes/selection evidence; read by `verify_freeze.py`. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes** | **KEEP** with freeze record |
| `models/frozen_stage23/load_frozen.py` | 5,434 | Seven-class Stage 23 loader; imported by `app/inference.py`, checked by tests and registry; currently depends on absent Stage 23 checkpoint and numerical-policy module. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes** | **KEEP** while app/freeze record exists |
| `models/frozen_stage23/model_registry.json` | 9,858 | Seven-class class order, checkpoint path/hash, validation split hash and evaluation policy; read by loader/verifier and cited in docs. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes** | **KEEP**; preserve exact bytes |
| `models/frozen_stage23/verify_freeze.py` | 3,313 | Read-only seven-class freeze verifier; referenced by README/tests and pinned by manifest. | HISTORICAL_SEVEN_CLASS | **Yes** | **KEEP** with freeze record |

`data/raw/HAM10000_metadata.csv` (563,277 B) is **REQUIRED_FOR_NINE_CLASS** as the seven-label source metadata and remains outside cleanup scope. The canonical binary tree `data/isic/data/` and its protected mirror remain outside cleanup scope. No binary patient/lesion metadata exists under `data/metadata/`; that directory is absent.

## Dependency and cleanup gate

`src/dataset.py`, `src/train.py`, `src/evaluate.py`, and `src/run_baseline.py` are seven-class/HAM-only consumers. `src/run_quality_analysis.py` and `src/run_uncertainty_analysis.py` depend on the old processed cache and leakage-aware split. Stage 23 tests and `app/inference.py` depend on historical freeze files and additional currently absent experiment modules/checkpoint; the baseline checkpoint cannot replace them. `docs/REPOSITORY_RESTRUCTURING_PLAN.md`, `docs/NINE_CLASS_DATA_PIPELINE.md`, `CURRENT_PROJECT_INVENTORY.md`, and `PROJECT_STATUS.md` contain historical references; some describe the split directory as present and are stale after its apparent deletion. No current source/test reference justifies using the old split CSVs for nine-class training.

**Recommended order after explicit approval:** first pin a complete backup/commit and verify remote blobs and local hashes; then finish the nine-class overlap/target protocol; only then consider archiving the naive split and sample previews. Keep the leakage-aware split provenance, processed cache/log, unique checkpoint, freeze registry, and nine-class audit records until each dependency and reproducibility claim is resolved. **Approved cleanup now: none.**
